# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file. See [standard-version](https://github.com/conventional-changelog/standard-version) for commit guidelines.

### 0.0.27 (2026-09-12)


### Features

* **corpus:** harvest vatican.va footnotes and wire new encyclical citations ([8b0e1b0](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/8b0e1b09a016df79b75382f89092fa3b02638a44))
* **corpus:** import 700+ papal documents from vatican.va ([cc88acc](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/cc88acc49a1ee8c2820d60563bef4bfb7c790d22))
* **i18n:** split UI and content locale, prefer official over AI ([6d55985](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/6d559853efba4a87893abf66b5853abb6aec2f24))
* Lectio divina diaria, Verbum Domini y recordatorios locales ([a81429b](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/a81429b612e58d35bb18a87aee97e2faa463e2eb))
* **lectio:** pack OLM citations offline and default VD reflection ([2a6e82e](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/2a6e82ef75c5d88a09f728ab7bda72aa9b934b38))
* **papacy:** pontiff pack from vatican.va with ficha and letter catalog ([4bb7bdc](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/4bb7bdc03cc37ef2997300bc24e360891e876444))
* **search:** document citation map, clickable refs, inline harvest ([9ad4b65](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/9ad4b65437c5d77c9b163ccce4d538580e643384))
* **search:** ship sca fixture and validate patristic verse-hit sidecar ([08d4960](https://github.com/RafaelAngelRamirez/documentos-vaticanos/commit/08d4960bcbe0a07a873e24aa7aa7b2cf080d7c6d))

### Docs

* **play:** closed-testing Console gate (ficha / App content / testers Alpha + n8n `PLAY_STATUS=completed`)
* **play:** AAB packaging notes (`-Xmx4096m`, FQCN MainActivity, Play 500 MB base-module cap)

### Bug Fixes

* **android:** Play `com.docvat` AAB — Gradle heap 4g, FQCN `MainActivity`, disable lint `Instantiatable`

### 0.0.26 (2026-09-12)

### Features

* **corpus:** harvest de notas vatican.va (`#fn` / `_ftn` / `_edn`) hacia `referencias[]` sin crear unidades; tope de 900 caracteres para no inflar el APK (*Verbum Domini* pasaba de 51 MB a ~0.2 MB)
* **corpus:** packs de lectura ES de Francisco y Benedicto XVI (*Laudato si'*, *Fratelli tutti*, *Deus caritas est*, *Spe salvi*, *Lumen fidei*, *Evangelii gaudium*, *Amoris laetitia*, *Caritas in veritate*, *Sacramentum caritatis*, *Dilexit nos*)
* **search:** códigos `LS`/`FT`/`DCE`/`SpS`/`LF`/`EG`/`AL`/`CiV`/`SCa`/`DN` (`Dn` sigue siendo Daniel); grafo documento↔documento; sidecar patrístico acotado
* **lectio:** Lectio divina diaria (`/lectio`) con recordatorios locales
* **lectio:** leccionario OLM empaquetado (Adviento–Pascua y solemnidades; citas de Felix Just / *Ordo Lectionum Missae*) y reflexión offline de *Verbum Domini* 87; Vatican News queda como overlay opcional
* **papacy:** catálogo de pontífices y documentos vatican.va
* **i18n:** idioma de interfaz y de textos independientes; catálogo prefiere edición oficial sobre IA
* **dev:** `ng serve` reescribe deep links (`/explorar/relaciones`) también con `Accept: */*`
* **android:** `targetSdk` / `compileSdk` 36 (Play) con AGP 8.9.1 y Gradle 8.11.1
